# casa-rs and CASA 6.7.6.14 tasks and tools

Truth class: generated reference
Generated by: `scripts/check-casa-inventory.py --write`
Sources: `resources/casa-compat/casa-6.7.6.14/` (read from CASA by `tools/casa-compat/dump_casa_task_inventory.py`), `crates/casa-provider-contracts/resources/parameter-surfaces.json`, `parameter-catalog.json`, the CASA class signatures in `crates/casars-python/python/tests/data/`, and reviewed `resources/casa-compat/semantics/`

> Do not edit this file by hand. Regenerate it after changing the inventory, a surface, a recorded class signature or a reviewed file.

Each casa-rs task that mimics a CASA task (#931) against that task as CASA 6.7.6.14 defines it: whether it presents CASA's signature, the CASA parameters casa-rs does not take yet, those it takes but accepts only at CASA's default, the parameters casa-rs takes that CASA does not (casa-rs names for CASA concepts, or casa-rs-only controls), whether the shared parameters keep CASA's order, and the defaults that differ. `calibrate`, `msconvert` and `msexplore` are casa-rs tasks with no CASA counterpart.

The CASA parameters casa-rs lacks or accepts only at their defaults are tracked by family: images in #977, MeasurementSet and flagging in #978, calibration in #979 and `tclean` in #980.

For direct `cBool`, `cFloat`, `cInt`, `cPath` and `cStr` schemas with the recorded coercions, a value both implementations reject for type reports CASA's wording, unless a narrower unimplemented-form diagnostic applies. Required paths, unions, vectors and variants retain casa-rs's diagnostics (#1099). CASA accepts booleans as integers and coerces them to floats; where casa-rs refuses one, it keeps its own message. Exception classes follow the casa-rs Python contract.

#### simobserve parameter implementation

Implementation status is **not behavioral verification**. Implemented means the stated scope has a native code path; Partial has known missing forms or combinations; Default only is derived from the runtime refusal contract; Not implemented has no implementation; Unassessed has not been reviewed. Evidence states are listed separately below. No signature or return-structure result establishes scientific equivalence.

Audit baseline: `49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947`. Status counts: Default only: 0, Implemented: 37, Not implemented: 0, Partial: 1, Unassessed: 0.

#### simobserve interacting modes and task behavior

<a id="simobserve-behavior-sky-model"></a>
##### Sky model and component list

**Partial** — skymodel through modifymodel and flatimage with inbright, indirection, incell, incenter and inwidth; complist with compwidth and comp_nchan, alone or with a sky model.

Compared in 6 of #920's 11 cases and by the skymodel fixture; the indirection and incell overrides and comp_nchan > 1 are not exercised against CASA's task.

Evidence: [task-tests](#simobserve-evidence-task-tests), [parity-20261103](#simobserve-evidence-parity-20261103).

<a id="simobserve-behavior-antennas"></a>
##### Antenna lists

**Partial** — antennalist and sdantlist as files, ALMA resolution strings ('alma;0.5arcsec'), the search through the project directory and CASA's alma/simmos, and sdant.

Configuration names need CASA's data directory: casa-rs does not ship alma/simmos (an open owner question on #1053).

Evidence: [task-tests](#simobserve-evidence-task-tests), [parity-20261103](#simobserve-evidence-parity-20261103).

<a id="simobserve-behavior-pointings"></a>
##### Pointings

**Partial** — setpointings with direction, mapsize, maptype and pointingspacing, or a ptgfile; written to <stem>.ptg.txt and checked against the model.

maptype='square' is compared only through the single-dish case.

Evidence: [task-tests](#simobserve-evidence-task-tests), [parity-20261103](#simobserve-evidence-parity-20261103).

<a id="simobserve-behavior-schedule"></a>
##### Calibrator and observing schedule

**Partial** — caldirection and calflux, integration, totaltime, refdate and hourangle.

The calibrator cases are compared with CASA's sm re-predict, since CASA's task leaves the calibrator unpredicted (decision). hourangle other than 'transit' and refdate is compared only at 2012/06/21.

Evidence: [task-tests](#simobserve-evidence-task-tests), [parity-20261103](#simobserve-evidence-parity-20261103).

<a id="simobserve-behavior-observation"></a>
##### Observation and prediction

**Partial** — obsmode 'int', 'sd' and '' (corrupting an existing MeasurementSet), outframe, and the prediction of the model and calibrator.

outframe other than LSRK is not compared.

Evidence: [task-tests](#simobserve-evidence-task-tests), [parity-20261103](#simobserve-evidence-parity-20261103).

<a id="simobserve-behavior-noise"></a>
##### Thermal noise and leakage

**Partial** — thermalnoise 'tsys-atm', 'tsys-manual' and '' with user_pwv, t_ground, t_sky, tau0, seed, leakage and correlator.

Noise is compared statistically (variances and KS tests), since draws differ; each environment parameter is exercised once; seed is casa-rs's own stream. CASA's own suite holds its noise tests to CASA's seed-11111 draws within 5%, which casa-rs's random stream cannot reproduce; over 20 seeds the two implementations' noise levels agree to 0.5%.

Evidence: [task-tests](#simobserve-evidence-task-tests), [parity-20261103](#simobserve-evidence-parity-20261103), [casa-suite-20261103](#simobserve-evidence-casa-suite-20261103), [noise-seeds-20261103](#simobserve-evidence-noise-seeds-20261103).

<a id="simobserve-behavior-outputs"></a>
##### Files, overwrite and graphics

**Partial** — CASA's file names under the project, overwrite, verbose and graphics.

graphics writes no .skymodel.png, .observe.png or .quick.psf yet and warns (#1025). An empty project and wrongly typed comp_nchan or sdant are refused, but not in CASA's words (#1053).

Evidence: [task-tests](#simobserve-evidence-task-tests), [parity-20261103](#simobserve-evidence-parity-20261103), [casa-suite-20261103](#simobserve-evidence-casa-suite-20261103).

#### simobserve deviations and unfinished work

##### compwidth-unset-spectral-increment: Previously agreed

- Record: [An unset `compwidth` gives the component-only model a meaningless spectral increment](https://github.com/bglenden/casa-rs/blob/49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947/docs/CASA%20%28C%2B%2B%29%20bugs.md#an-unset-compwidth-gives-the-component-only-model-a-meaningless-spectral-increment)
- Inputs: simobserve with a component list, no sky image and compwidth=''.
- CASA: It observes 2 GHz when compwidth is not a quantity, but sets the .compskymodel spectral increment from compwidth anyway, yielding a meaningless value (the RA in radians read as hertz, 4.976 Hz for RA 19h, in CASA 6.7.6).
- casa-rs: Uses the 2 GHz simobserve observes with.
- Rationale: The increment is the model's only spectral information beyond its reference frequency.
- User impact: The component-only model carries a consistent spectral increment.
- Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
- Evidence: [bugs-record](#simobserve-evidence-bugs-record).

##### calibrator-last-pass-scans-lists: Previously agreed

- Record: [simobserve's calibrator: the last pass, its scans and two component lists](https://github.com/bglenden/casa-rs/blob/49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947/docs/CASA%20%28C%2B%2B%29%20bugs.md#simobserves-calibrator-the-last-pass-its-scans-and-two-component-lists)
- Inputs: simobserve with a phase calibrator (caldirection and calflux), also with a complist or totaltime ending inside a pass.
- CASA: CASA 6.7.6 never observes the pointings of an incomplete last pass (a 300 s total on 105 pointings has only the calibrator's scan), selects fields range(0,nfld) so the calibrator's scans keep zeros plus noise, and joins component lists into one nonexistent table so predict returns False.
- casa-rs: Observes the pointings the time allows, predicts every scan including the calibrator's, and predicts both lists as one list of their components.
- Rationale: The task means to observe and predict all of these.
- User impact: CASA data can lack pointings, have unpredicted calibrator scans, or be all zero; casa-rs data are complete (parity cases agree at 5e-9 NRMS).
- Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
- Evidence: [bugs-record](#simobserve-evidence-bugs-record).

##### noisier-t-cal-left: Previously agreed

- Record: [simobserve leaves `<stem>.noisier.T.cal`](https://github.com/bglenden/casa-rs/blob/49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947/docs/CASA%20%28C%2B%2B%29%20bugs.md#simobserve-leaves-stemnoisiertcal)
- Inputs: simobserve with tsys-atm noise set after leakage (the noisier measurement set).
- CASA: The setnoise table is written beside the noisy MeasurementSet, and the clean-up deletes <stem>.noisy.T.cal and <stem>.noisy.sd.T.cal but not <stem>.noisier.T.cal.
- casa-rs: Writes no Tsys table for any of the three.
- Rationale: The table is a by-product the task means to remove.
- User impact: CASA leaves a stray table in the project that casa-rs never creates.
- Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
- Evidence: [bugs-record](#simobserve-evidence-bugs-record).

##### observing-loop-never-ends: Previously agreed

- Record: [simobserve's observing loop never ends for pointings of no length](https://github.com/bglenden/casa-rs/blob/49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947/docs/CASA%20%28C%2B%2B%29%20bugs.md#simobserves-observing-loop-never-ends-for-pointings-of-no-length)
- Inputs: simobserve with integration='0s' or a pointing file of zero times.
- CASA: The while (sttime-scanstart) < totalsec loop advances by each pointing's scan length; when a pass's lengths sum to zero the time never advances and CASA loops forever, collecting pointings.
- casa-rs: Refuses such a schedule with a message.
- Rationale: A schedule with no length cannot be observed.
- User impact: The task stops with an error instead of hanging.
- Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
- Evidence: [bugs-record](#simobserve-evidence-bugs-record).

##### skymodel-deleted-when-output: Previously agreed

- Record: [simobserve deletes a sky model that is the model it writes](https://github.com/bglenden/casa-rs/blob/49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947/docs/CASA%20%28C%2B%2B%29%20bugs.md#simobserve-deletes-a-sky-model-that-is-the-model-it-writes)
- Inputs: simobserve with skymodel given as the full path <project>/<stem>.skymodel (e.g. a rerun on an earlier run's model) and overwrite=True.
- CASA: The output is the input: CASA deletes the model and then fails to read it.
- casa-rs: Refuses the input, and the model is left in place.
- Rationale: The user's input must not be destroyed.
- User impact: The model survives and the user gets a clear refusal.
- Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
- Evidence: [bugs-record](#simobserve-evidence-bugs-record).

##### project-path-nests-in-itself: Proposed

- Record: [simobserve nests a project path inside itself](https://github.com/bglenden/casa-rs/blob/49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947/docs/CASA%20%28C%2B%2B%29%20bugs.md#simobserve-nests-a-project-path-inside-itself)
- Inputs: simobserve with project given as a path, e.g. 'a/b'.
- CASA: The task names files fileroot + '/' + project + '.' + cfg, so 'a/b' gives a/b/a/b.<cfg> in a directory that does not exist; only a plain name in the working directory works.
- casa-rs: Names files by the directory's last component, a/b/b.<cfg>.
- Rationale: For a plain name the two are the same.
- User impact: Project paths work in casa-rs and fail in CASA.
- Owner decision: Pending; not an accepted deviation.
- Evidence: [bugs-record](#simobserve-evidence-bugs-record).

##### symlink-in-project-write-through: Previously agreed

- Record: [simobserve writes through a symbolic link in the project](https://github.com/bglenden/casa-rs/blob/49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947/docs/CASA%20%28C%2B%2B%29%20bugs.md#simobserve-writes-through-a-symbolic-link-in-the-project)
- Inputs: simobserve overwrite=True in a project containing a symbolic link such as <stem>.ptg.txt.
- CASA: CASA copies ptgfile through the link, replacing the file the link points to outside the project, and creates outside files for dangling links; links to tables stop the task via rmtree; existence checks follow links.
- casa-rs: Replaces a link in the project with the new file or table, leaves the target alone, and counts a link as existing under overwrite=False.
- Rationale: Owner's decision of 2026-10-26: only the project's own files are replaced.
- User impact: Files outside the project are no longer overwritten or created through links.
- Owner decision: Owner decision, 2026-10-26: the project's own files are the only ones overwrite replaces (recorded with the provider contracts' overwrite exception).
- Evidence: [bugs-record](#simobserve-evidence-bugs-record).

##### direction-list-of-one-mean: Proposed

- Record: [simobserve rounds a `direction` list of one to its mean](https://github.com/bglenden/casa-rs/blob/49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947/docs/CASA%20%28C%2B%2B%29%20bugs.md#simobserve-rounds-a-direction-list-of-one-to-its-mean)
- Inputs: simobserve with direction=['J2000 18h00m00.123456789s ...'] (a list of one) versus the same direction as a string.
- CASA: CASA passes the list to calc_pointings2, and direction_splitter centres it at its mean written at precision 5, so the map is laid out about 1e-5 s of RA (0.15 mas) from the string case.
- casa-rs: casa-rs's simutil follows CASA, but simobserve treats a list of one like a string and lays the map out at the exact direction.
- Rationale: The task contract resolves a string to a list of one, and the rounding carries no information.
- User impact: A map is placed at the exact direction, differing from CASA by about 0.15 mas for a list of one.
- Owner decision: Pending; not an accepted deviation.
- Evidence: [bugs-record](#simobserve-evidence-bugs-record).

#### simobserve evidence

Evidence is scoped to its named revision, inputs and comparator. Historical reported results need applicability review; planned or unexecuted tests are not passes.

<a id="simobserve-evidence-signature"></a>
##### signature

**Signature/default inventory — recorded.** CASA 6.7.6.14 names and defaults; not behavior.

- [resources/casa-compat/casa-6.7.6.14/tasks/simobserve.json](https://github.com/bglenden/casa-rs/blob/49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947/resources/casa-compat/casa-6.7.6.14/tasks/simobserve.json)

<a id="simobserve-evidence-task-tests"></a>
##### task-tests

**casa-rs task tests — recorded.** The task's stages, files, refusals and messages, without CASA.

- [crates/casa-simulation/tests/simobserve.rs](https://github.com/bglenden/casa-rs/blob/49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947/crates/casa-simulation/tests/simobserve.rs)
- [crates/casa-simulation/src/simobserve/tests.rs](https://github.com/bglenden/casa-rs/blob/49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947/crates/casa-simulation/src/simobserve/tests.rs)

<a id="simobserve-evidence-parity-920"></a>
##### parity-920

**CASA-paired task runs (PR-reported) — superseded.** All 11 cases agree: DATA NRMS 6.2e-8 to 2.0e-7 for the interferometer cases, 1.05e-6 for leakage, calibrators against CASA's sm re-predict at 5.2e-9 and 5.5e-9; noise KS p 0.11 to 0.92. CASA 6.7.6-14's task_simobserve.py, casa-rs at 29f0b87a6e. Not re-run at a current revision; CASA's own test_task_simobserve cases are not compared. 11 cases: VLA and ALMA image models, a component-only model with tsys-atm, leakage with both noise modes, calibrators, single dish and a pointing file.

<a id="simobserve-evidence-parity-20261103"></a>
##### parity-20261103

**CASA-paired task runs — passed.** All 11 cases agree: interferometer DATA NRMS 6.2e-8 to 2.9e-7 and leakage 1.05e-6 (limit 1e-3); calibrators against CASA's sm re-predict 5.3e-9 and 5.5e-9; single dish 8.7e-9; noise variances within 1 percent of unity on both sides (KS p 0.11 to 0.92); the coarse-cell case refused with CASA's message. CASA's own test_task_simobserve cases are not compared. #920's 11 cases: VLA and ALMA image models, a component-only model with tsys-atm, leakage with both noise modes, calibrators, single dish, a pointing file and a refused coarse cell.

- [tools/simulation/simobserve_parity.py](https://github.com/bglenden/casa-rs/blob/49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947/tools/simulation/simobserve_parity.py)
- [tools/simulation/compare_sm_ms.py](https://github.com/bglenden/casa-rs/blob/49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947/tools/simulation/compare_sm_ms.py)
- Revision: `49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947`; CASA: `casatasks 6.7.6.14, casatools 6.7.6-14`.
- Command: `CASAPY tools/simulation/simobserve_parity.py --out OUT --casars-bin TARGET/release/simobserve --casatestdata /Volumes/home/casatestdata --atmosphere-model TARGET/release/examples/at_tool_quantities (bg-mac-mini, CPU)`.
- Receipt: `resources/casa-compat/evidence/simobserve-parity-20261103.json`.

<a id="simobserve-evidence-casa-suite-20261103"></a>
##### casa-suite-20261103

**CASA's test_task_simobserve with casa-rs in place of CASA's task — measured.** Of the 54 cases CASA 6.7.6.14 passes on this host, casa-rs passes 45. Of the other 9, 3 refuse as CASA does in casa-rs's own words: an empty project, and comp_nchan and sdant of the wrong type. The other 6 are noise levels just outside the suite's 5% band: tsys-manual interferometer noise 5.8% below CASA's recorded value, and tsys-atm single-dish noise 5.05% above it in all five variants. Those six are CASA's own seed-11111 draws, which casa-rs's random stream cannot reproduce; over 20 seeds the levels agree to 0.5% (noise-seeds-20261103). 23 cases fail with CASA's own task too and 16 are skipped by both. CASA's own simobserve suite (93 cases): sky models, component lists, both, noise, bad inputs and correlators, judged by the suite's checks against casatestdata's reference products.

- [tools/simulation/simobserve_casa_suite.py](https://github.com/bglenden/casa-rs/blob/d5c88b52b718e4b3834447724d66371f269a0083/tools/simulation/simobserve_casa_suite.py)
- Revision: `49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947`; CASA: `casatasks 6.7.6.14, casatools 6.7.6-14`.
- Command: `CASAPY tools/simulation/simobserve_casa_suite.py --test-file CASA6/casatasks/tests/tasks/test_task_simobserve.py --casatestdata LOCAL_COPY --casars-bin TARGET/release/simobserve --out OUT (bg-mac-mini)`.
- Receipt: `resources/casa-compat/evidence/simobserve-casa-suite-20261103.json`.

<a id="simobserve-evidence-noise-seeds-20261103"></a>
##### noise-seeds-20261103

**CASA-paired noise levels over 20 seeds — passed.** The suite's two noise cases, 20 seeds each: casa-rs's mean level over CASA's is 1.002 for testNZ_sdAtm (tsys-atm single dish) and 1.005 for testNZ_intMan (tsys-manual interferometer), within 0.2 and 0.9 standard errors. The levels CASA's suite records are CASA's own seed-11111 draws, 1.8 and 2.9 of CASA's standard deviations from its mean, which a different random stream cannot reproduce: they explain the suite's six noise failures. testNZ_sdAtm and testNZ_intMan of CASA's test_task_simobserve, the suite's noise level (noisy minus original, real part, standard deviation) per run.

- Revision: `d5c88b52b718e4b3834447724d66371f269a0083`; CASA: `casatasks 6.7.6.14, casatools 6.7.6-14`.
- Command: `CASAPY tools/simulation/simobserve_noise_seeds.py --casatestdata LOCAL_COPY --casars-bin TARGET/release/simobserve --out OUT (bg-mac-mini)`.
- Receipt: `resources/casa-compat/evidence/simobserve-noise-seeds-20261103.json`.

<a id="simobserve-evidence-refusals-1099-20261104"></a>
##### refusals-1099-20261104

**CASA simobserve refusal comparisons — measured.** CASA's testBad_comp_nchan, testBad_sdant and test_noProject each pass with both CASA and casa-rs. The substring filter also selected testBad_sdantlist, which failed in both controls because no sky input was found; it is not counted as a comparison. A separate CASA probe records the seed type refusal, and the Python binding agrees. Integer type refusal messages and the empty project path; exception classes retain the #931 casa-rs decision.

#### tclean parameter implementation

Implementation status is **not behavioral verification**. Implemented means the stated scope has a native code path; Partial has known missing forms or combinations; Default only is derived from the runtime refusal contract; Not implemented has no implementation; Unassessed has not been reviewed. Evidence states are listed separately below. No signature or return-structure result establishes scientific equivalence.

Audit baseline: `c64f13cb09054b080fe8b228a5f2c02c99bfd486`. Status counts: Default only: 5, Implemented: 46, Not implemented: 0, Partial: 40, Unassessed: 1.

Mappings, defaults, catalog domains, constraints and conditional default-only refusals are generated from the existing task contract. The catalog may admit values that the native reader then refuses: the reviewed scope and linked combinations describe those restrictions.

| CASA parameter | CASA default | casa-rs default; mapping | Implementation | Scope and runtime restrictions | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `vis` | `''` | `''`; `vis` → `vis` | Partial | Contract 38: a list of MeasurementSets images as one, as CASA's tclean images its vis list. Each is opened and surveyed with its own selectors (one per MeasurementSet, or one or a list of one for all; another length is refused with CASA's message), ordered stably by its earliest MAIN TIME and visited as CASA visits its ms0, ms1, ... keys, as text (input_parameters.checkAndFixSelectionPars, mslisthelper.sort_mslist, imager_base.selectData); one selecting no sample is left out unless it is the first (SynthesisImagerVi2::selectData). The image's coordinates (obs date, telescope, phase centre, rest frequency, a cube's axis) come from CASA's msobj (buildCoordinateSystem): the first MeasurementSet, or for a cube or cubedata whose start is a frequency or velocity the last one whose selected band holds it (CAS-12877); a continuum band, and so the MFS and MT-MFS reference frequency, spans every one. A cube reads each other MeasurementSet's window where it reaches the cube, and one that does not reach it is read so its MODEL_DATA holds the zero prediction, as CASA writes it. Weighting density spans every MeasurementSet; datacolumn falls back to DATA per MeasurementSet; savemodel writes each one's MODEL_DATA (or virtual model). CASA's addwtspec, which adds WEIGHT_SPECTRUM to a listed MeasurementSet lacking it as a Vi/VB2 workaround, is not done: casa-rs reads each MeasurementSet's own weights, which that column copies, and never modifies its inputs. A row's spectral window and field are keyed among every MeasurementSet's, as if they were concatenated, so the mosaic keeps each one's beams (CASA's msId_spw, SimplePBConvFunc::convIndex) and a mosweight density each one's fields (VisImagingWeight's msId_field) apart; its dishes are every MeasurementSet's aperture classes (HetArrayConvFunc::findAntennaSizes). Refused with several MeasurementSets (#980): the awproject gridder (its catalog keys cells on the first MeasurementSet's frequencies), a moving phase centre, specmode cubesource, fitspw and a cube's per-channel density weighting. A cube with an unset start reads the band selected over every MeasurementSet, on the first one's channels (SynthesisParamsImage::buildCoordinateSystem). #### tclean interacting modes and task behavior

<a id="tclean-behavior-selection"></a>
##### Selection and multi-MS inputs

**Partial** — vis, field, spw, timerange, uvrange, antenna, scan, observation, intent, datacolumn and selectdata (#54).

IDs/ranges, names/simple patterns, mixed field lists, SPW/channel text, intent and UV limits are routed natively. Sixteen field cases compare exact physical MAIN row order or refusal with CASA, including flagged and padded FIELD metadata. Since the main imaging cutover was integrated (3016f1b), FIELD selection resolves over the observation catalogue's FIELD rows on MSv2 and MSv4 alike; on the production traversal the field cases of the CASA row oracle (IDs, names, glob, mixed, range) and both refusals read exactly CASA's physical MAIN rows. Production T1 compares ID/name selections with selectdata=False on the single native route. Scan and observation selection is active from tclean contract 33 (owner decision 2026-11-02): on that path scan 1, scan 3 and observation 0 read exactly CASA's rows, and T1 images each numeric selection as its equivalent FIELD selection with either selectdata value and refuses CASA's refused forms without writing images. Through the public Python task, 13 science cases and 23 CASA grammar cases pass against CASA on CPU64, CPU32 and Metal32 (108/108). CODE/numeric-name fallback, advanced field forms, richer scan/observation grammar, remain unfinished; a vis list images as one observation from contract 38, as CASA's tclean does (awproject with several MeasurementSets is refused, #980); antenna and timerange are active from contract 34 (timerange on MeasurementSets only). Image-science comparisons for selected real datasets, flags, channel membership and data-column fallback remain separate required evidence.

Evidence: [reader](#tclean-evidence-reader), [representative](#tclean-evidence-representative), [field-selection](#tclean-evidence-field-selection), [cloud-continuation](#tclean-evidence-cloud-continuation), [scan-observation-activation](#tclean-evidence-scan-observation-activation), [vis-list-20261102](#tclean-evidence-vis-list-20261102).

<a id="tclean-behavior-restart"></a>
##### Restart, product persistence and recalculation

**Partial** — imagename, restart, startmodel, calcres, calcpsf, restoration; main and outlier fields (#944).

Contract 36 continues every spectral mode, Taylor family and Stokes set of the main image and activates calcres/calcpsf (CASA test-case matrix at 4fe0e56: 11/48 restart/calcres/calcpsf cases pass, from 1/48 counting the 74a3b85 run's CASA refusals as environment failures; the rest are blocked by other gaps or an f32 scalar floor). Earlier: paired CASA 6.7.6.14 small standard Stokes-I MFS defaults pass (18 native 10->0->10 rows, 6 persisted nontrivial-mask cases, 6 CASA-to-native and 2 native-to-CASA continuations). Embedded model/mask pixel masks, tabulated spectral grids, new explicit/auto masks over occupied masks when cleaning, outliers, and reads under mosaic/A-projection/facets remain unfinished; concurrency at one prefix unassessed. CASA mutates prior logtables whereas native preserves them (owner-agreed). New-image history completeness remains unfinished. This is not general persistence or full metadata acceptance.

Evidence: [reader](#tclean-evidence-reader), [lifecycle](#tclean-evidence-lifecycle), [restart-native-current](#tclean-evidence-restart-native-current), [approved-comparisons-restart](#tclean-evidence-approved-comparisons-restart), [scalars-empty-mask](#tclean-evidence-scalars-empty-mask), [numbering-preview](#tclean-evidence-numbering-preview), [numbering-activation](#tclean-evidence-numbering-activation), [continuation-20261102](#tclean-evidence-continuation-20261102).

<a id="tclean-behavior-spectral"></a>
##### Cube axes, reference frequencies and moving sources

**Partial** — specmode, spw, start, width, nchan, reffreq, restfreq, outframe, veltype, interpolation and perchanweightdensity.

nchan=-1 takes CASA's count from the start or the first selected channel to the band's far edge in channel, frequency and radio-velocity grids, and interpolation='cubic' fits casacore's four-point cubic (#1066); an optical-velocity grid, which CASA tabulates, is refused (#1096); a cube over several selected windows lays its axis over their channels merged as CASA's MSTransformRegridder::combineSpwsCore merges them, grids every window's rows onto it, and with nchan=-1 counts a channel grid's planes over the merged band as regridChanBounds does (#980); MFS outframe (LSRK/BARY/TOPO, header only, gridding stays LSRK as in CASA) and restfreq lists (first active, the others listed in the header) are read; MFS REST outframe and blank restfreq entries remain restricted. Moving-source cubesource needs its own representative oracle (#714). MVC removal is a separate prior owner decision, not a passing moving-source comparison. The moving-MFS spectral envelope now uses CASA FIELD-origin direction semantics; six fixed/moving CPU64/CPU32/GPU32 comparisons pass recorded WCS values, and the previous 3.5 Hz increment mismatch is repaired at its unchanged tolerance. This does not complete moving-PB or cubesource acceptance. The ordinary MFS default SOURCE rest-frequency label is now repaired; five fresh mosaic/AW weight families and small restart metadata checks match. DOPPLER-backed SOURCE lookup and multiple SOURCE key groups with stored wildcards are explicitly unfinished, not approved deviations. The MFS header's first visibility field is the least (ARRAY_ID, DATA_DESC_ID, FIELD_ID) of the selected samples, CASA's buffer order: the MSv2 adapter states each partition's array and the survey reads the rows' arrays for a partition holding several (3bdf6fa, after Astra's review found the merge had dropped ARRAY_ID); a T1 run with opposing arrays records CASA's choice. An MSv4 has no arrays. The moving-MFS increment oracle on the catalogue path gives CASA's 125020996.11132812 Hz exactly (8b0fe44).

Evidence: [reader](#tclean-evidence-reader), [representative](#tclean-evidence-representative), [mvc-decision](#tclean-evidence-mvc-decision), [taylor-pb-mfs](#tclean-evidence-taylor-pb-mfs), [approved-comparisons-restart](#tclean-evidence-approved-comparisons-restart), [cloud-continuation](#tclean-evidence-cloud-continuation), [cube-axes-20261103](#tclean-evidence-cube-axes-20261103).

<a id="tclean-behavior-gridders"></a>
##### Gridder, weighting and beam combinations

**Partial** — standard/W/mosaic/AW, rectangular geometry, facets, normtype, beam terms and density weighting.

Rectangular standard/W/mosaic and uvtaper were implemented in #970. AW remains square/equal-cell, and images MFS, MT-MFS and cubes whose selected windows hold one channel each (each plane its own weight image, issue 719; a window of several, issue 1121); it reads tclean's aterm, psterm, wbawp, conjbeams and any positive wprojplanes (#980), and an imported CASA cache must already exist: where cfcache names a missing directory CASA fills it, casa-rs refuses (aw_cf_source=native-evla generates its own). Odd cells an older CASA wrote (test_mosaic_mtmfs's staged caches) are read about CASA's convOrigin, shape/2+1; on those cases the PSF agrees to 1e-7 but CASA's weight image is 0.8% above casa-rs's in scale, and CASA's second call into the same image leaves weight.tt1 and weight.tt2 undivided by sumwt; both are open (#980). Mosaic per-field density weighting (mosweight) is implemented, continuum and a cube's per-channel density (BriggsCubeWeightor multiField, #818); W/AW facets and additional gridders/weightings remain incomplete. Native CF generation is verified separately from CASA cache oracles. The native write_pb default is True. Homogeneous ALMA-labelled 7m arrays select ACA; legacy VLA PB now covers all named bands and NVSS frequency gaps. Fresh ordinary-default W/Hogbom20 CPU64/CPU32 runs pass all seven products, with PB NRMS zero. EVLA/VLA mosaics grid with CASA's common EVLA primary beam (HetArrayConvFunc COMMONPB, PBMath1DEVLA) at the first buffer's LSRK frequencies and MosaicFT's sector edges; conjbeams on one-plane mosaics grids with CASA's widened, resampled conjugate cell (#980). Moving/nonconstant FIELD directions, mixed dish classes, unsupported telescopes and remaining EVLA bands are unfinished. Explicit GPU W/AW remains refused. CASA Float channel-frequency narrowing is now matched in shared Taylor factors. All five fresh mosaic/native-AW MT-MFS rows pass the owner-approved scoped weak weight.tt1 rule and retained original recorded checks; 3 override only raw weight.tt1 relative-error failures. Exact guards and 1e-6 matrix-scale limits remain. Full metadata is separate. Existing #690 odd-sumwt scaling remains distinct. Legacy VLA cube PB takes CASA's channel-zero aperture scaled per channel: both T1.5 VLA cube rows give .pb NRMS 0.0 at 9768bb1, 3016f1b and 2628d8b (pre-candidate 0.0136); independent review pending. Cube Briggs density cells now use CASA's negated u and v (2628d8b): all 3,056,967 point-cube-briggs (row, channel) weights match CASA's to 1.35e-7, where 7 exact Float ties previously read a neighbouring cell.

Evidence: [reader](#tclean-evidence-reader), [additions](#tclean-evidence-additions), [representative](#tclean-evidence-representative), [default-products](#tclean-evidence-default-products), [compensated-current](#tclean-evidence-compensated-current), [taylor-pb-mfs](#tclean-evidence-taylor-pb-mfs), [approved-comparisons-restart](#tclean-evidence-approved-comparisons-restart), [cloud-continuation](#tclean-evidence-cloud-continuation).

<a id="tclean-behavior-deconvolution"></a>
##### Deconvolution, masks and stopping

**Partial** — Hogbom, Clark, multiscale, MT-MFS, iteration controls, user masks and auto-multithresh.

The owner approved contract31's native CASA-inclusive Hogbom default, preserving explicit strict. Normal-build ordinary Hogbom10 point-source comparisons pass seven products on CPU64/CPU32/GPU32 with no explicit accounting option; prototype ALMA mosaic CLEAN also passes in that mode. Independent default-continuation and migration tests pass. ASP, MEM, Clark-Stokes, MT-MFS nterms=1, PB masks/pbsquare, several auto-mask controls, explicit restoring beams are incomplete or refused. The shared nmajor=-1/0/positive domain is implemented;20 small paired CLI/Python rows verify initial-only products, threshold/zero-mask precedence, seed retention and exact counts. Compare convergence/flux/beam and exact mask support separately; nonlinear component identity is not required, but approved mode-specific gates stay binding. The owner accepted only the original identity-bound b6a87 clean8/cpu32 weak model result; raw model.tt1 NRMS 0.0012759712 remains recorded. No general weak-model waiver or automatic future-output acceptance exists. Explicit all-zero image masks now stop with CASA code 7 for the verified standard-MFS cases; existing nonzero models are preserved on empty-mask restart. Broader mode combinations still require evidence. Signed integer pixel-box clipping and explicit empty CRTF are repaired at086a67f1d4.36 CLI pairs pass recorded products/exact counts at two PB limits on three backends;2 public Python pairs additionally pass two selected scalars and old-file preservation under fresh numbering. General regions and other mask combinations remain unfinished. A plane whose published .sumwt is positive but at most CASA's 1e-07 bound is now empty as in CASA (zero PSF, residual, model and image; nothing cleaned), verified by a paired constructed-weight cube probe (8fcee92).

Evidence: [reader](#tclean-evidence-reader), [representative](#tclean-evidence-representative), [compensated-current](#tclean-evidence-compensated-current), [adopted-defaults](#tclean-evidence-adopted-defaults), [standard-mtmfs-current](#tclean-evidence-standard-mtmfs-current), [approved-comparisons-restart](#tclean-evidence-approved-comparisons-restart), [scalars-empty-mask](#tclean-evidence-scalars-empty-mask), [mask-boxes](#tclean-evidence-mask-boxes), [nmajor-zero](#tclean-evidence-nmajor-zero), [cloud-continuation](#tclean-evidence-cloud-continuation).

<a id="tclean-behavior-returns"></a>
##### Return values, exceptions and metadata

**Partial** — Python and CLI CASA return record, output topology, coordinates, beams, mask validity, model-column effects and reopen.

The recorded 22-key structure exists. Final maxpsfsidelobe and raw automatic cyclethreshold now report already-measured controller statistics with CASA Float rounding; 18 small pinned cases pass these two scalar checks, exact iteration/major/stop integers and recorded scientific products on CPU64/CPU32/Metal32. This does not verify every return key. summaryminor's rows are measured for each plane, and agree with CASA on 29 of 35 cases, the rest attributed (summary-rows-20261103). The fd7b8e7b2d full normal gate passes 4,698 workspace tests/45 skips and relevant checks. Each image holds tclean's history and CASA's object name, image type and miscinfo but memreq and memavail (image-history-20261103). Broader exceptions/mode combinations and representative persistence remain open. Earlier moving-MFS increment and ordinary SOURCE rest-frequency defects are repaired at their recorded revisions. Contract32 numbering adds20 small product rows, with19 raw selected-scalar passes and one identity-bound owner-accepted Metal cyclethreshold result (1.5132ppm difference). The original failed verdict remains; no future-output waiver. The owner agreed to native preservation of prior histories during fresh numbering; correct new-image history remains required. Nmajor-zero adds20 scoped product/count pairs; two public Python pairs also gate the two selected numeric scalars. Full return parity remains unfinished. The owner declined the native .summary.json sidecar (2026-11-02): a tclean run writes only its image products, as CASA's does, and the six-case replay now requires an exact product inventory; casars-imager writes the run summary only when CASA_RS_IMAGER_RUN_SUMMARY=1, for performance tooling. The handoff's point-cube-briggs maxpsfsidelobe raw failure (relative 8.2e-6 at 2851c6dc, 9768bb1 and 3016f1b) is attributed to those 7 boundary weights and passes at 2628d8b (relative 7.7e-8), with all six handoff cases passing both selected scalars.

The scoped matrix-scaled weak weight.tt1 gate is approved and all 5 fresh mosaic/native-AW rows pass; 3 raw NRMS failures remain visible as scoped overrides. Contract32 False is now active and all20 small public numbering product checks pass. One Metal threshold output is owner-accepted by identity-bound review; its raw scalar failure stays visible, with no future waiver. Combined normal gate at086a passes4710 workspace tests/45skips and all normal checks. Scientific accumulation is unchanged by those two slices. No numeric rule or solver arithmetic changed. #### tclean deviations and unfinished work

##### interactive-headless: Agreed for now

- Inputs: interactive=True; graphical mask editing and pause/resume.
- CASA: Uses casaviewer where available; 6.7.6.14 on macOS raises when the viewer is absent.
- casa-rs: Refuses interactive=True explicitly; accepts False. Supply a mask or use supported auto-multithresh controls.
- Rationale: The application is headless; the owner accepted the exception for the current phase.
- User impact: No graphical mask editing/pause-resume. Future interactive operation remains wanted, not permanently out of scope.
- Owner decision: Owner, 2026-10-31, this chat: Headless exception for now, but ultimately we will want to do something for this. Recorded on #980.
- Evidence: [interactive-decision](#tclean-evidence-interactive-decision), [reader](#tclean-evidence-reader).

##### mvc: Previously agreed

- Inputs: specmode='mvc'.
- CASA: Supports the MVC task path.
- casa-rs: Refuses MVC; cubesource is the surviving moving-source path.
- Rationale: Reuse owner decision B on #656, reflected in #714; do not revive a deleted mode without a new decision.
- User impact: MVC workflows are unavailable; cubesource representative acceptance remains unfinished.
- Owner decision: Decision B on #656; #714 non-goal: no MVC revival.
- Evidence: [mvc-decision](#tclean-evidence-mvc-decision), [reader](#tclean-evidence-reader).

##### restart: Unfinished

- Inputs: Any occupied main/outlier image namespace; restart=False or recalculation controls.
- CASA: Continues existing products by default, or chooses a numbered fresh prefix with restart=False.
- casa-rs: Bounded same-grid standard Stokes-I MFS default continuation now has native gates, small paired CASA 10->0->10/mask evidence and both-direction continuation. Contract32 activates single-main fresh numbering; small public products/preservation pass. Other occupied namespaces, coordinated outlier/orphan numbering and calc flags remain unfinished.
- Rationale: Reuse the existing native model/mask lifecycle with admitted ownership, preserving unsupported-state refusals while completing parity.
- User impact: The bounded native default path can continue prior work; unsupported modes and flags still require completion and are not agreed exclusions.
- Owner decision: Pending; not an accepted deviation.
- Evidence: [lifecycle](#tclean-evidence-lifecycle), [reader](#tclean-evidence-reader), [restart-native-current](#tclean-evidence-restart-native-current), [approved-comparisons-restart](#tclean-evidence-approved-comparisons-restart), [numbering-activation](#tclean-evidence-numbering-activation).

##### uvtaper-units: Previously agreed

- Inputs: A unitless uvtaper entry.
- CASA: Interprets bare values as radians, despite documenting lambda; large values may zero every imaging weight.
- casa-rs: Requires explicit angle or lambda/klambda units and errors clearly on bare values.
- Rationale: Prevent a documented-unit mismatch from silently producing an empty image.
- User impact: Callers must supply units; numerical unit-bearing taper behavior remains governed by #970 evidence.
- Owner decision: Owner, 2026-10-31, this chat: Require explicit units.
- Evidence: [additions](#tclean-evidence-additions), [reader](#tclean-evidence-reader).

##### startmodel-stokes: Previously agreed

- Inputs: Start-model Stokes labels differ from the target; equal plane counts.
- CASA: Copies planes by position without converting polarization, since only direction and spectral axes are regridded.
- casa-rs: Copies equal-count planes by position and warns; focused native warning/permuted-plane tests pass, and the single Q-to-I case is paired with CASA 6.7.6.14. Multi-plane paired and unequal-count behavior remain unverified.
- Rationale: Match CASA's behavior, while making the label mismatch visible.
- User impact: A Q model can seed I and permuted Stokes planes are copied positionally; this is not a physical polarization conversion. Unequal-plane-count behavior still needs oracle assessment.
- Owner decision: Owner, 2026-10-31, this chat: Match but issue a warning. This supersedes the prior refusal.
- Evidence: [additions](#tclean-evidence-additions), [reader](#tclean-evidence-reader), [default-products](#tclean-evidence-default-products).

##### restart-absolute-directory: Previously agreed

- Inputs: restart=False with an absolute imagename and existing products.
- CASA: 6.7.6.14 incrementImageNameList slices dirname[2:], corrupting an absolute directory before creating the next numbered image; the probe fails to create its PSF.
- casa-rs: Activated native contract32 keeps the correct absolute directory; alternating absolute/relative public calls choose and publish the expected fresh prefixes.
- Rationale: Avoid a reproducible CASA path-handling bug while preserving the intended numbering behavior.
- User impact: Numbered products stay in the requested directory. Broader restart acceptance remains open.
- Owner decision: Owner, 2026-10-31, this chat: Use the correct absolute directory. Recorded on #944.
- Evidence: [lifecycle](#tclean-evidence-lifecycle), [numbering-preview](#tclean-evidence-numbering-preview), [numbering-activation](#tclean-evidence-numbering-activation).

##### native-write-pb-default: Previously agreed

- Inputs: Ordinary casa-rs tclean calls that omit the casa-rs-only write_pb option; profiles from contract 29 or earlier.
- CASA: Ordinary supported tclean imaging creates a .pb product; CASA has no write_pb parameter.
- casa-rs: Contract 30 defaults the extra write_pb option to True. Explicit False remains an opt-out; migrating an omitted old default warns and adopts True. All 92 CASA parameters/defaults and pbcor=False are unchanged.
- Rationale: Make ordinary product production agree with CASA without patching CASA or changing its parameter defaults.
- User impact: The PB file is now produced within implemented PB scope. Moving-field, telescope and frequency limitations remain unfinished work and can currently require explicit False.
- Owner decision: Owner, 2026-10-31, this chat: Approve the casa-rs default change.
- Evidence: [default-products](#tclean-evidence-default-products), [reader](#tclean-evidence-reader).

##### hogbom-default: Previously agreed

- Inputs: deconvolver='hogbom' with a positive minor-cycle iteration limit.
- CASA: CASA 6.7.6.14 may commit one extra component while clamping its reported count to the requested limit.
- casa-rs: Contract31 defaults the native extra policy to casa-inclusive; explicit strict remains available. Omitted older profile defaults warn and adopt the new value, while explicit values are preserved.
- Rationale: Match CASA's reported count and scientific products using the existing implementation.
- User impact: Positive Hogbom cycles may apply one extra component while reporting the CASA count. Other solvers retain their canonical strict inactive policy. No CASA parameter/default changes.
- Owner decision: Owner, 2026-10-31, this chat: Approved both proposed adoptions, including CASA-compatible Hogbom counting by default.
- Evidence: [compensated-current](#tclean-evidence-compensated-current), [adopted-defaults](#tclean-evidence-adopted-defaults).

##### weak-taylor-weight-comparison: Previously agreed

- Inputs: Two-term Stokes-I MFS mosaic/AW weight.tt1; both reference weak-coupling measures<=1e-3, with all reviewed accumulator guards.
- CASA: An almost cancelling odd weight term makes error relative to its own near-zero norm unstable; exact CASA product values remain the oracle.
- casa-rs: The approved gate uses E2 across valid pixels and Emax on reference-only PB support, each<=1e-6 of the Taylor matrix scale. All other original checks remain. Three raw NRMS failures are visible scoped overrides; outputs are unchanged.
- Rationale: Judge a weak coupling against its surrounding positive Taylor matrix while preserving PSD, masks, finiteness and other scientific checks.
- User impact: An odd term below the matrix-scale error budget could be lost and still pass this one criterion. No model, metadata or arbitrary-mode waiver.
- Owner decision: Owner, 2026-11-01, this chat: Approve the scoped comparison rule.
- Evidence: [approved-comparisons-restart](#tclean-evidence-approved-comparisons-restart).

##### specific-weak-mtmfs-model-output: Previously agreed

- Inputs: Only original b6a87 clean8/cpu32 512-square standard MT-MFS result bound by the owner-decision receipt and payload identities.
- CASA: The weak model.tt1 coefficient is -0.00026645659818314016 Jy; the original raw comparison remains available.
- casa-rs: The bound coefficient is -0.0002667965891305357 Jy, raw NRMS 0.0012759712. The owner accepted this exact output: ideal published intensity differs by at most 5.7469e-7 in the recorded common-frame MFS interval, with all 18 other original product checks passing.
- Rationale: A mathematically checked component solve and exact support show that this specific coefficient discrepancy has a much smaller represented in-band intensity effect.
- User impact: The raw failure remains; this exact reviewed output is accepted. Future outputs have no automatic approval, and runtime visibility prediction remains separately unverified.
- Owner decision: Owner, 2026-11-01, this chat: Accept this specific reviewed output.
- Evidence: [standard-mtmfs-current](#tclean-evidence-standard-mtmfs-current), [approved-comparisons-restart](#tclean-evidence-approved-comparisons-restart).

##### numbering-metal-cyclethreshold: Previously agreed

- Inputs: Only unchanged99b cli-gpu32/explicit_startmodel output and bound raw receipt/input/binary identities.
- CASA: Final cyclethreshold0.07878141850233078 Jy/beam.
- casa-rs: 0.07878153771162033 Jy/beam; raw original scalar comparison fails by1.5132ppm. Formula matches measured native state; upstream residual cause unproved.
- Rationale: Owner accepted this exact output under2e-6 relative+1e-8 Jy/beam with all other recorded checks retained; engineering choice, not a universal Float error bound.
- User impact: Accepts0.119209microJy/beam in this returned threshold only; changes no values/defaults/algorithm and grants no future-output waiver.
- Owner decision: Owner2026-11-01, this chat: Accept this specific output. Raw failed verdict retained.
- Evidence: [numbering-activation](#tclean-evidence-numbering-activation).

##### numbered-prior-history: Previously agreed

- Inputs: restart=False fresh numbered outputs with older matching-prefix images.
- CASA: Appends later task history to all old matching image logtables; paired runs change only old logtable/table.dat and table.f0.
- casa-rs: Leaves old files unchanged byte for byte, excluding locks.
- Rationale: The owner agreed preserving independent older results instead of appending history from later fresh-numbering calls.
- User impact: Old histories omit later calls creating other images. Correct history on each new image remains required and unassessed.
- Owner decision: Owner2026-11-01, this chat: Preserve prior histories. Correct new-image history is not waived.
- Evidence: [numbering-activation](#tclean-evidence-numbering-activation).

<a id="tclean-evidence-cube-axes-20261103"></a>
##### cube-axes-20261103

**CASA tclean test-case matrix, ad hoc derived cube axes and cubic cubes, test_cube sweep, T0/T1 laws — measured: 18/18 cube-axes cases pass (worst NRMS 8.8e-7), cubic beside a flagged channel included; test_cube 53/55 with test_cube_22 passing and test_cube_13 refused naming #1096; a linear cube's time and peak memory unchanged within noise on CPU and Metal.** tclean's nchan=-1 with a channel selection, start or width (MSTransformRegridder::regridChanBounds with nchan<0, in channel, frequency and radio-velocity grids), strided channel selections and interpolation='cubic' (casacore InterpolateArray1D's four-point cubic when gridding and predicting). Every product of the 18 cases agrees with CASA to 8.8e-7 NRMS with exact iteration counts and scalars, cubic with natural and per-channel Briggs weights and beside a flagged channel included, and CASA's failure for width=-2 with no start is a refusal on both sides. CASA's test_cube sweep is unchanged but for test_cube_13, whose optical-velocity grid CASA tabulates (#1096). Native: casa-ms regrid laws pinned to CASA's nchan=-1 axes, operator laws for the cubic stencil, gridding, flags, weights, prediction and halo, the Metal-against-CPU laws over four-point native records, and T1 cubes (nchan=-1, cubic).

<a id="tclean-evidence-pb-statistics-20261103"></a>
##### pb-statistics-20261103

**CASA tclean ad hoc cases against a baseline build, main-matrix regression cases, T0 laws — measured: 5 of 7 new cases pass and fail at the baseline; 1 passes at both; 1 matches counts and products and misses cyclethreshold by 2.2e-4 relative (attributed); regression 37/39, the 2 recorded divergences.** tclean's statistics inside the primary beam (#1078, #1079): the residual's pixel mask (pb > pblimit) for its peaks and automatic mask, and calcRobustRMS's pixels for the nsigma noise, for every gridder. Native: T0 beam-statistics laws and a counting law for the beam's charge.

<a id="tclean-evidence-mtmfs-polarized-review-20261103"></a>
##### mtmfs-polarized-review-20261103

**CASA tclean test-case matrix, casa-rs's own polarized MT-MFS cases, T1 imaging laws — passed.** tclean deconvolver='mtmfs' of polarized Taylor families after #1104's review (#1065). #1065's 14 cases pass as before (13 of 14, worst NRMS 5.3e-6; test_stokes_mtmfs_IQUV.1+2 refused by usemask='pb', #1061). Of casa-rs's own cases, refim_point_linRL's polarized point in IQUV, and in IQUV with restoringbeam='common' (one beam recorded, as CASA records it), passes every product; in IV every product but a tt1 model that is rounding in both. The refim_mawproject mosaic in IQUV failed (zero Q and U PSF moments), so Stokes planes other than I alone are refused beyond the standard gridder (issue 1112). The T1 law a_polarized_taylor_family_is_imaged_on_its_own_stokes_plane puts a circularly polarized point's V on V's plane of each term.

<a id="tclean-evidence-common-beam-platform-20261105"></a>
##### common-beam-platform-20261105

**Common-beam quantity conversion against CASA on macOS and Linux — measured: source-confirmed conversion matches CASA references on both platforms.** The enclosing ellipse uses casacore Quantum::getValue's division by the unit ratio. Separate CASA cases on both platforms verify that division order and arcsecond construction, and each law fails its targeted mutation on both. One other CASA result differs between platforms because of its iterative floating-point enclosure; the native result is compared with the local platform's CASA.

- [resources/casa-compat/evidence/tclean-common-beam-cross-platform-1140-20261105.json](https://github.com/bglenden/casa-rs/blob/585223403b91d078a938c33450bc057f12a19110/resources/casa-compat/evidence/tclean-common-beam-cross-platform-1140-20261105.json)

<a id="tclean-evidence-blank-mask-beam-20261105"></a>
##### blank-mask-beam-20261105

**Blank PSF beams and common-beam automasking against CASA — measured: full 11-channel CASA and native mask cubes match; the parent differs.** Blank PSF planes inherit the largest fitted beam from the full image domain before a channel block finds its common beam. CASA and native match all 8,910,000 mask pixels in the cross-block restart; the parent differs at 177. CASA common-beam matrix order and ellipse construction are tested, including polarized and unit-sensitive cases. A fresh T1.5 case and representative T2 numerical comparison pass; T2 raw beam metadata differs from CASA within tolerance and is unchanged from the parent. Issue 1140 records the review and mutation proofs.

## Tools

Each CASA class casa-rs presents in Python, against the signatures recorded from CASA 6.7.6.14 (`crates/casars-python/python/tests/data/casa_<class>_signatures.json`), which the Python tests hold the classes to. A reviewed class has a file under `resources/casa-compat/semantics/tools/` giving each method's status, scope and evidence; the others are not reviewed yet. The simulation classes (`simulator`, `componentlist`, `measures`, `quanta` and `simutil`) are reviewed in #1053, and `image`, `coordsys`, `table`, `msmetadata` and `ms` in #1025.

| Class | Python | CASA methods | Reviewed | Implemented | Partial | Not implemented | Disabled in CASA | Deprecated in CASA | Unassessed |
|---|---|---:|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| [componentlist](#componentlist) | `casars.tools` | 61 | yes | 19 | 0 | 42 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| coordsys | `casars.tools` | 69 | no | | | | | | |
| image | `casars.tools` | 103 | no | | | | | | |
| [measures](#measures) | `casars.tools` | 48 | yes | 24 | 0 | 24 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| ms | `casars.tools` | 77 | no | | | | | | |
| msmetadata | `casars.tools` | 103 | no | | | | | | |
| [quanta](#quanta) | `casars.tools` | 57 | yes | 26 | 0 | 31 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| [simulator](#simulator) | `casars.tools` | 33 | yes | 18 | 11 | 0 | 3 | 1 | 0 |
| [simutil](#simutil) | `casars.simutil` | 57 | yes | 46 | 2 | 9 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| table | `casars.tools` | 83 | no | | | | | | |

### componentlist

#### componentlist method implementation

Implementation status is **not behavioral verification**. Implemented means the stated scope has a native code path; Partial has known missing modes or combinations, which raise `NotImplementedError` naming the issue shown; Not implemented raises it for every call; Disabled in CASA and Deprecated in CASA do what CASA does; Unassessed has not been reviewed. Evidence states are listed separately below.

Audit baseline: `49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947`. Status counts: Implemented: 19, Partial: 0, Not implemented: 42, Disabled in CASA: 0, Deprecated in CASA: 0, Unassessed: 0.

| CASA method | CASA parameters | Implementation | Scope | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `add` | `thecomponent, iknow=True` | Not implemented (#1053) | Raises NotImplementedError naming #1053. CASA's simulation tasks and simutil do not call it. | [signature](#componentlist-evidence-signature), [refusals](#componentlist-evidence-refusals) |
| `addcomponent` | `flux=[], fluxunit='Jy', polarization='Stokes', dir=[], shape='point', majoraxis=[], minoraxis=[], positionangle=[], freq=[], spectrumtype='constant', index=[], optionalparms=[0.0], label=''` | Implemented | Adds point, Gaussian and disk components with constant or spectral-index spectra, call by call as CASA 6.7.6.14 does (`casa_componentlist_tool.json`); shapes CASA cannot construct raise and add nothing. | [signature](#componentlist-evidence-signature), [casa-values](#componentlist-evidence-casa-values), [casa-on-casa](#componentlist-evidence-casa-on-casa) |
| `asciitocomponentlist` | `filename, asciifile, refer='J2000', format='ST', direction={}, spectrum={}, flux={}, log=True` | Not implemented (#1053) | Raises NotImplementedError naming #1053. CASA's simulation tasks and simutil do not call it. | [signature](#componentlist-evidence-signature), [refusals](#componentlist-evidence-refusals) |

#### componentlist interacting modes and tool behavior

#### componentlist deviations and unfinished work

##### limb-darkened-disk-no-exponent: Previously agreed

- Record: [Writing a limb-darkened disk without an exponent aborts CASA](https://github.com/bglenden/casa-rs/blob/49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947/docs/CASA%20%28C%2B%2B%29%20bugs.md#writing-a-limb-darkened-disk-without-an-exponent-aborts-casa)
- Inputs: cl.fromrecord for a limb-darkened disk without optionalParameters, then cl.close.
- CASA: writeTable stores an empty optional-parameter vector and throws casacore::indexError from the ComponentList destructor, terminating the process (CASA 6.7.6: close aborts); readTable would read out of bounds.
- casa-rs: Leaves the cell undefined, read as the shape's default exponent 0.05.
- Rationale: The default exponent is what every undescribed row already reads as.
- User impact: Such lists save and reload instead of aborting the process.
- Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
- Evidence: [bugs-record](#componentlist-evidence-bugs-record).

##### single-spectral-index-read-past-end: Previously agreed

- Record: [A single spectral index is read past its end](https://github.com/bglenden/casa-rs/blob/49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947/docs/CASA%20%28C%2B%2B%29%20bugs.md#a-single-spectral-index-is-read-past-its-end)
- Inputs: cl.addcomponent(spectrumtype='spectral index', index=alpha) on a polarised component, then predict.
- CASA: The matrix sampleStokes used by every component prediction has no one-index branch and reads indices 1 to 3 out of bounds; the per-frequency version scales only I (read from source, not probed).
- casa-rs: Reads a single index as [alpha,0,0,0], so fractional polarisation is constant.
- Rationale: This is what the matrix sampler's comments describe.
- User impact: Polarised components with one index predict Q, U, V reliably instead of scaled by whatever follows the vector.
- Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
- Evidence: [bugs-record](#componentlist-evidence-bugs-record).

##### addcomponent-four-flux-as-stokes: Previously agreed

- Record: [`cl.addcomponent` stores four flux values as Stokes](https://github.com/bglenden/casa-rs/blob/49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947/docs/CASA%20%28C%2B%2B%29%20bugs.md#claddcomponent-stores-four-flux-values-as-stokes)
- Inputs: cl.addcomponent(flux=[1,0.1,0.2,0.9], polarization='circular').
- CASA: setflux stores four values with FluxRep::setValue(Vector<Double>), which resets the representation to Stokes, so the values become I,Q,U,V rather than RR,RL,LR,LL (probed with CASA 6.7.6).
- casa-rs: Does the same (owner decision 2026-10-23), including raising for an unknown name or a non-Stokes representation with one value.
- Rationale: Scripts get the list CASA would give them.
- User impact: A circular or linear four-value flux is silently read as Stokes in both implementations.
- Owner decision: Owner decision, 2026-10-23 (#841), recorded in docs/CASA (C++) bugs.md.
- Evidence: [bugs-record](#componentlist-evidence-bugs-record).

##### addcomponent-flux-single-precision: Proposed

- Record: [`cl.addcomponent` stores the flux in single precision](https://github.com/bglenden/casa-rs/blob/49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947/docs/CASA%20%28C%2B%2B%29%20bugs.md#claddcomponent-stores-the-flux-in-single-precision)
- Inputs: cl.addcomponent(flux=0.1).
- CASA: setflux reads values into a Vector<Complex> (single precision), so flux=0.1 is stored as 0.10000000149011612 (CASA 6.7.6).
- casa-rs: Keeps the values given.
- Rationale: Avoids a needless single-precision rounding.
- User impact: Fluxes differ from CASA's by at most 6e-8 relative.
- Owner decision: Pending; not an accepted deviation.
- Evidence: [bugs-record](#componentlist-evidence-bugs-record).

##### shape-axes-recorded-units: Proposed

- Record: [The `cl` tool records a shape's axes in the units it was given](https://github.com/bglenden/casa-rs/blob/49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947/docs/CASA%20%28C%2B%2B%29%20bugs.md#the-cl-tool-records-a-shapes-axes-in-the-units-it-was-given)
- Inputs: cl.addcomponent(majoraxis='1arcsec', ...) then reading the shape record.
- CASA: TwoSidedShape remembers the units its axes were set in and records them so ({'unit':'arcsec','value':1.0}); a shape read from a table records arcmin axes and degree position angle.
- casa-rs: Holds radians and records every shape as CASA records one read from a table (arcmin, 0.016666666666666666).
- Rationale: The quantities are the same, and the table is the same either way.
- User impact: The record's unit and value differ from the input's but denote the same quantity.
- Owner decision: Pending; not an accepted deviation.
- Evidence: [bugs-record](#componentlist-evidence-bugs-record).

##### addcomponent-point-for-bad-shape: Previously agreed

- Record: [`cl.addcomponent` keeps a point for a shape it cannot make](https://github.com/bglenden/casa-rs/blob/49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947/docs/CASA%20%28C%2B%2B%29%20bugs.md#claddcomponent-keeps-a-point-for-a-shape-it-cannot-make)
- Inputs: cl.addcomponent with a misspelled shape name, a limb-darkened disk, or an axis that is not an angle.
- CASA: addcomponent ignores setshape's False return, so the component stays a point and addcomponent returns True (CASA 6.7.6).
- casa-rs: Does the same and logs at ERROR; what setshape raises, addcomponent raises too after removing the component.
- Rationale: Scripts that misspell a shape get points, as in CASA.
- User impact: A misspelled shape yields a point source instead of an error.
- Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA's behaviour is deliberate or users' results depend on it, casa-rs follows it.
- Evidence: [bugs-record](#componentlist-evidence-bugs-record).

### measures

#### measures method implementation

Implementation status is **not behavioral verification**. Implemented means the stated scope has a native code path; Partial has known missing modes or combinations, which raise `NotImplementedError` naming the issue shown; Not implemented raises it for every call; Disabled in CASA and Deprecated in CASA do what CASA does; Unassessed has not been reviewed. Evidence states are listed separately below.

Audit baseline: `49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947`. Status counts: Implemented: 24, Partial: 0, Not implemented: 24, Disabled in CASA: 0, Deprecated in CASA: 0, Unassessed: 0.

|---|---|---|---|---|

#### measures interacting modes and tool behavior

#### measures deviations and unfinished work

##### measures-tool-default-frame: Proposed

- Record: [The measures tool converts with a frame at MJD 0 and the geocentre](https://github.com/bglenden/casa-rs/blob/49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947/docs/CASA%20%28C%2B%2B%29%20bugs.md#the-measures-tool-converts-with-a-frame-at-mjd-0-and-the-geocentre)
- Inputs: me.measure(me.direction('J2000','12h00m00','45d00m00'),'AZEL') in a fresh measures tool, with no doframe call.
- CASA: The tool starts with a default frame (MJD 0 UTC, zero-vector position, J2000 pole) and converts using it without a warning, returning AZEL for 1858-11-17 at the Earth's centre.
- casa-rs: Starts with an empty frame, so the conversion fails with 'Cannot convert due to missing frame information' until doframe sets the element.
- Rationale: A conversion should not silently use placeholder epoch and position.
- User impact: A script that forgot doframe gets an error rather than a meaningless answer.
- Owner decision: Pending; not an accepted deviation.
- Evidence: [bugs-record](#measures-evidence-bugs-record).

##### iau2000-keeps-iau1980-sidereal: Previously agreed

- Record: [IAU 2000 keeps the IAU 1980 sidereal time and ecliptic obliquities](https://github.com/bglenden/casa-rs/blob/49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947/docs/CASA%20%28C%2B%2B%29%20bugs.md#iau-2000-keeps-the-iau-1980-sidereal-time-and-ecliptic-obliquities)
- Inputs: Conversions to HADEC, AZEL, ITRF, MECLIPTIC or TECLIPTIC with measures.iau2000.b_use set.
- CASA: casacore still forms GAST from IAU 1982 GMST and the IAU 1980 equation of the equinoxes, and uses IAU 1980 obliquities (fundArg T^2 term -0.0059 arcsec, 0.37 mas in MECLIPTIC by 2026).
- casa-rs: Computes all of these as casacore does (casacore_sidereal, casacore_frames).
- Rationale: Follows casacore for parity; it moved IAU 2000A J2000 to HADEC from 20.4 to 16.7 mas off casacore.
- User impact: Results under IAU 2000 match casacore, including its remaining IAU 1980 pieces.
- Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA's behaviour is deliberate or users' results depend on it, casa-rs follows it.
- Evidence: [bugs-record](#measures-evidence-bugs-record).

##### direction-latitude-unnormalized: Previously agreed

- Record: [Direction latitudes come from an unnormalized vector](https://github.com/bglenden/casa-rs/blob/49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947/docs/CASA%20%28C%2B%2B%29%20bugs.md#direction-latitudes-come-from-an-unnormalized-vector)
- Inputs: Direction conversions that use the galactic or FK4 matrices, e.g. B1950 to GALACTIC or J2000 to B1950 near a pole.
- CASA: The 10-digit literal matrices are orthogonal only to about 7e-11 and doConvert does not renormalize, so getLat's asin(z) is off by (|v|-1)tan(b) and becomes NaN once z exceeds 1.
- casa-rs: Converts with the same matrices, renormalizes and reports the latitude of the unit vector.
- Rationale: asin(z) assumes a unit vector.
- User impact: Reported angles differ by up to 51 microarcsec (1.6 mas at b=-89.77 deg) while directions agree to 0.01 microarcsec.
- Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
- Evidence: [bugs-record](#measures-evidence-bugs-record).

##### iau2000-jmean-j2000-order: Previously agreed

- Record: [IAU 2000 JMEAN to J2000 applies bias and precession in the wrong order](https://github.com/bglenden/casa-rs/blob/49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947/docs/CASA%20%28C%2B%2B%29%20bugs.md#iau-2000-jmean-to-j2000-applies-bias-and-precession-in-the-wrong-order)
- Inputs: MECLIPTIC or TECLIPTIC to J2000 conversion under IAU 2000.
- CASA: deapplyPrecession applies B then P giving P*B*v', not the inverse B*P*v', so JMEAN to J2000 is not the inverse of J2000 to JMEAN (0.14 mas off in 2026, growing).
- casa-rs: Inverts exactly.
- Rationale: The reverse transform must invert the forward one.
- User impact: Round trips through the IAU 2000 ecliptics differ from casacore by 0.14 mas in 2026.
- Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
- Evidence: [bugs-record](#measures-evidence-bugs-record).

##### iau2000-gmst1-tt-fraction: Previously agreed

- Record: [IAU 2000 GMST1 adds the TT day fraction](https://github.com/bglenden/casa-rs/blob/49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947/docs/CASA%20%28C%2B%2B%29%20bugs.md#iau-2000-gmst1-adds-the-tt-day-fraction)
- Inputs: UT1 to GMST1 (and so LMST) under IAU 2000.
- CASA: UT1_GMST1 adds GMST00/(2pi), already a sidereal time of day, to the TT epoch, counting the TT day fraction twice: UT1 51544.5 gives GMST1 58258.280 against 58257.779, and GMST1_UT1 does not undo it.
- casa-rs: Uses the IAU 1976 GMST1 under either model, as casacore's own GAST does.
- Rationale: This keeps GMST1, LMST, GAST and LAST consistent with each other.
- User impact: LMST differs from casacore's IAU 2000 value but agrees with casacore under IAU 1976.
- Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
- Evidence: [bugs-record](#measures-evidence-bugs-record).

Every implemented method, with CASA's messages.

### quanta

#### quanta method implementation

Implementation status is **not behavioral verification**. Implemented means the stated scope has a native code path; Partial has known missing modes or combinations, which raise `NotImplementedError` naming the issue shown; Not implemented raises it for every call; Disabled in CASA and Deprecated in CASA do what CASA does; Unassessed has not been reviewed. Evidence states are listed separately below.

Audit baseline: `49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947`. Status counts: Implemented: 26, Partial: 0, Not implemented: 31, Disabled in CASA: 0, Deprecated in CASA: 0, Unassessed: 0.

#### quanta interacting modes and tool behavior

#### quanta deviations and unfinished work

##### quantities-non-conforming-units: Previously agreed

- Record: [The tools convert quantities whose units do not conform](https://github.com/bglenden/casa-rs/blob/49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947/docs/CASA%20%28C%2B%2B%29%20bugs.md#the-tools-convert-quantities-whose-units-do-not-conform)
- Inputs: integrationtime='10Jy', integrationtime='10m' or freq='1s' passed to a simulator method; a direction string such as 'J2000 12h00m00 garbage'.
- CASA: CASA 6.7.6 converts without checking conformance: '10Jy' becomes 1e-25 s, '10m' 10 s, '1s' 1 Hz, and an unreadable direction token keeps the previous value (declination 180 deg).
- casa-rs: Refuses both with casacore's 'From/to units not consistent' or a parse error, while keeping CASA's cross-dimension conversions (number is SI, angle is time, length is frequency via c).
- Rationale: Non-conforming units are almost certainly user errors and should not become silent wrong values.
- User impact: Scripts with a wrong unit now stop with an error instead of running with a nonsense value.
- Owner decision: Standing rule, AGENTS.md "CASA deviations" (owner, #1054): where CASA crashes, aborts, reads out of bounds, loses or corrupts data, or contradicts its documentation or evident intent, casa-rs corrects it.
- Evidence: [bugs-record](#quanta-evidence-bugs-record).

#### quanta evidence

Evidence is scoped to its named revision, inputs and comparator. Historical reported results need applicability review; planned or unexecuted tests are not passes.

**Signature inventory — recorded.** CASA 6.7.6.14's methods, names and defaults, and the object-surface bundle's; not behavior.

- [crates/casars-python/python/tests/test_tools_quanta.py](https://github.com/bglenden/casa-rs/blob/49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947/crates/casars-python/python/tests/test_tools_quanta.py)

**CASA-probed values — passed.** 96 of the 131 tests pass against CASA 6.7.6.14's own qa tool: every test of a value or a raised message. The 35 others test what only casa-rs has (its refusals, bundle and import) or one message CASA writes to its own log; the receipt classifies each. casa-rs passes the same tests in CI. Every implemented method, with CASA's messages.

- [crates/casars-python/python/tests/test_tools_quanta.py](https://github.com/bglenden/casa-rs/blob/49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947/crates/casars-python/python/tests/test_tools_quanta.py)

### simulator

#### simulator method implementation

Implementation status is **not behavioral verification**. Implemented means the stated scope has a native code path; Partial has known missing modes or combinations, which raise `NotImplementedError` naming the issue shown; Not implemented raises it for every call; Disabled in CASA and Deprecated in CASA do what CASA does; Unassessed has not been reviewed. Evidence states are listed separately below.

Audit baseline: `49c810c57210127fc4bc4f66289cadec90f49947`. Status counts: Implemented: 18, Partial: 11, Not implemented: 0, Disabled in CASA: 3, Deprecated in CASA: 1, Unassessed: 0.

| `close` |  | Implemented | Writes and closes the MeasurementSet, within the session's memory budget. CASA aborts here after a mosaic predict; casa-rs does not (decision). | [signature](#simulator-evidence-signature), [binding-tests](#simulator-evidence-binding-tests), [observe-20261103](#simulator-evidence-observe-20261103) |

| `setpa` | `mode='calculate', table='', interval=[]` | Disabled in CASA | Logs CASA's error and returns False, as CASA 6.7.6.14 does. | [signature](#simulator-evidence-signature), [binding-tests](#simulator-evidence-binding-tests) |
| `setpointingerror` | `epjtablename='', applypointingoffsets=False, dopbcorrection=False` | Disabled in CASA | Logs CASA's error and returns False, as CASA 6.7.6.14 does. | [signature](#simulator-evidence-signature), [binding-tests](#simulator-evidence-binding-tests) |
| `setseed` | `seed=185349251` | Implemented | The random seed; casa-rs's draws differ from CASA's, compared statistically. | [signature](#simulator-evidence-signature), [binding-tests](#simulator-evidence-binding-tests), [noise-20261103](#simulator-evidence-noise-20261103), [corruptions-20261103](#simulator-evidence-corruptions-20261103) |
| `setspwindow` | `spwname='XBAND', freq=[], deltafreq=[], freqresolution=[], refcode='TOPO', nchannels=1, stokes='RR LL'` | Implemented | A spectral window, keeping each window's frame (decision). | [signature](#simulator-evidence-signature), [binding-tests](#simulator-evidence-binding-tests), [observe-20261103](#simulator-evidence-observe-20261103), [scripts](#simulator-evidence-scripts) |
| `settimes` | `integrationtime=[], usehourangle=True, referencetime=[]` | Implemented | Integration time, reference time and hour-angle use. | [signature](#simulator-evidence-signature), [binding-tests](#simulator-evidence-binding-tests), [observe-20261103](#simulator-evidence-observe-20261103), [scripts](#simulator-evidence-scripts) |

Refused: mode='test', CASA's debugging ramp. | [signature](#simulator-evidence-signature), [binding-tests](#simulator-evidence-binding-tests), [troposphere-20261103](#simulator-evidence-troposphere-20261103), [refusals](#simulator-evidence-refusals) |

Refused: a voltage-pattern table, and dovp=True for other telescopes. | [signature](#simulator-evidence-signature), [binding-tests](#simulator-evidence-binding-tests), [predict-image-20261103](#simulator-evidence-predict-image-20261103), [refusals](#simulator-evidence-refusals) |
